Crucially, this decoupled design ensures excellent generalization capabilities, allowing seamless integration with different off-the-shelf models and robust adaptation across new datasets without requiring computationally expensive joint retraining. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks (ETH-UCY, SDD, and NBA) demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms strong single-model and ensemble baselines, achieving an average improvement of 10.5%, showcasing the effectiveness of scene-aware selective learning.

</details>


### [334] [Prior-Agnostic Robust Forecast Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24517)
*Zhi Chen,Cheng Peng,Wei Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究先验无关的鲁棒预测聚合问题，提出了一种简单的对数几率聚合器（log-odds aggregator），在三种知识场景下提供了（近乎）紧的极小化最大后悔界，并首次在已知状态空间{0,1}下实现了严格低于0.0226的后悔上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒预测聚合工作大多假设已知二元状态空间{0,1}，但实际中聚合者既不知信息结构，也不知完整先验甚至状态空间。本文旨在解决先验和状态空间均未知的鲁棒聚合问题。

Method: 提出一种简单显式的闭式对数几率聚合器（log-odds aggregator），在logit空间线性池化预测；分别在条件独立（CI）、Blackwell序结构与一般信息结构下分析其极小化最大后悔界。

Result: 1) 未知状态空间下CI结构后悔下界大于已知状态空间情形，所提聚合器达到0.0255的后悔；2) 已知状态空间{0,1}下CI结构后悔严格低于0.0226；3) 已知边际分布时，广义log-odds规则达到0.0228后悔，与下界0.0225互补。

Conclusion: 本文首次给出在已知状态空间下实现严格低于0.0226后悔上界的显式闭式聚合器，并系统刻画了未知状态空间下的聚合困难程度及紧致界。

Abstract: Robust forecast aggregation combines the predictions of multiple information sources to perform well in the worst case across all possible information structures. Previous work largely focuses on settings with a known binary state space, where the state is either 0 or 1. We study prior-agnostic robust forecast aggregation in which the aggregator observes only experts' reports, yet is ignorant of both the underlying joint information structure and the full prior, including the underlying state space. Unlike the standard model that fixes the binary state space {0, 1}, we allow the (binary) unknown state values to be arbitrary numbers in [0, 1], so the same reported probability may correspond to very different realized outcome frequencies across environments.
  Our main contribution is a simple, explicit, closed-form log-odds aggregator that linearly pools forecasts in logit space, together with (nearly-)tight minimax-regret guarantees across three knowledge regimes. We first show that under conditionally independent (CI) signals, robust aggregation with an unknown state space is strictly harder than in the known-state setting by establishing a larger lower bound, and our aggregation rule can achieve a worst-case regret of 0.0255. Along the way, we also characterize tight regret bounds for Blackwell-ordered structures and for general information structures. In the classical setting with known state space {0,1}, our aggregator achieves regret strictly below 0.0226 for CI structures. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first explicit closed-form aggregator that achieves a regret upper bound strictly less than 0.0226. Finally, we extend the model where the aggregator additionally knows each expert's marginal forecast distribution; in this setting, with the CI structures, we show that a generalized log-odds rule achieves regret of 0.0228, complementing with a lower bound of 0.0225.

</details>


### [335] [Stochastic simultaneous optimistic optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24537)
*Michal Valko,Alexandra Carpentier,Rémi Munos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出StoSOO算法，在未知函数局部光滑性的情况下，通过乐观策略和层次分区构建置信上界，实现带噪声的全局函数最大化，性能接近已知光滑性的专用算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有带状方法（如Kleinberg等，2008；Bubeck等，2011a）需要预先知道函数的半度量（局部光滑性度量），这在实践中往往难以获得。本文旨在消除这一先验知识需求，设计一种在未知光滑性条件下仍能高效进行全局优化的算法。

Method: 提出StoSOO算法，采用乐观策略，通过层次化分区函数定义域，迭代构建上置信界（UCB）来决定下一个采样点。算法仅假设函数在某个全局最大值附近关于某个半度量是局部光滑的，但无需知道该半度量具体形式。

Result: 有限时间分析表明，StoSOO在未知局部光滑性条件下，性能几乎与已知光滑性的专用最优算法相当。

Conclusion: StoSOO成功消除了对函数局部光滑性先验知识的需求，在更弱假设下实现了与专用算法相媲美的全局优化性能，拓展了带状方法在实际问题中的适用性。

Abstract: We study the problem of global maximization of a function f given a finite number of evaluations perturbed by noise. We consider a very weak assumption on the function, namely that it is locally smooth (in some precise sense) with respect to some semi-metric, around one of its global maxima. Compared to previous works on bandits in general spaces (Kleinberg et al., 2008; Bubeck et al., 2011a) our algorithm does not require the knowledge of this semi-metric. Our algorithm, StoSOO, follows an optimistic strategy to iteratively construct upper confidence bounds over the hierarchical partitions of the function domain to decide which point to sample next. A finite-time analysis of StoSOO shows that it performs almost as well as the best specifically-tuned algorithms even though the local smoothness of the function is not known.

</details>


### [336] [Dialysis Risk Prediction and Treatment Effect Estimation for AKI patients using Longitudinal Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24547)
*Kalyani P. Pande,Evan Yang,Bryan Zhu,Sandeep K. Mallipattu,Alisa Yurovsky,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用Transformer因果模型分析EHR数据，预测透析/ESRD风险并评估药物因果效应，发现ACE/ARB有保护作用，袢利尿剂有恶化作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透析或终末期肾病是罕见但重要的临床结局，临床医生需要了解药物暴露如何影响下游风险。

Method: 构建固定窗口EHR队列（90天观察，730天预测；N=81401），使用基于Transformer的因果多头模型，通过反事实暴露移除和插入估计药物层面和成分层面的平均处理效应（ATEs）。

Result: 测试集AUC为0.694，PR-AUC为0.094，在阈值0.883下F1得分为0.201，Brier得分为0.018。因果分析显示ACE/ARB有部分保护方向支持，袢利尿剂显示出恶化方向信号。

Conclusion: 模型能有效利用EHR数据进行药物因果效应评估，ACE/ARB对肾脏有保护作用，袢利尿剂可能增加风险。

Abstract: Progression to dialysis or end-stage renal disease is a rare but clinically important outcome. Clinicians need evidence on how medication exposures influence downstream risk. We constructed a fixed-window EHR cohort (90-day observation, 730-day prediction; N=81401; dialysis/ESRD prevalence: 1.1%) and modeled sequences of diagnoses, procedures, and medications with kidney laboratory trends (creatinine, BUN, eGFR). A transformer-based causal multi-head model was trained to estimate drug- and ingredient-level average treatment effects (ATEs) using counterfactual exposure removal and insertion under a full medication history setup. On test set, predictive performance reached an AUC of 0.694 and PR-AUC of 0.094. At the selected decision threshold (0.883), the model achieved an F1 score of 0.201 with a Brier score of 0.018. Post-hoc causal analyses of lab changes (eGFR, creatinine, BUN) using IPTW, AIPW, naive, and covariate-adjusted OLS methods assessed clinical directionality. Results showed partial protective-direction support for ACE/ARB exposures and worsening-direction signals for loop diuretics.

</details>


### [337] [Efficient learning by implicit exploration in bandit problems with side observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24555)
*Tomas Kocak,Gergely Neu,Michal Valko,Remi Munos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对部分可观测的在线学习问题（介于全信息和bandit反馈之间），提出了首个无需事先知晓观测系统即可实现近最优遗憾保证的算法，并通过隐式探索策略在组合优化场景中实现了计算与信息效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线学习算法在处理部分可观测反馈（如依赖观测系统的定向信息）时，要么需要提前知道观测结构，要么遗憾保证不理想。本文旨在设计一种无需先验知识、同时具备近最优遗憾的通用算法。

Method: 1. 提出首个不依赖观测系统先验知识的近最优遗憾算法；2. 针对组合优化问题，设计另一种始终计算高效的算法（需更精细的调参机制）；3. 引入一种名为"隐式探索"（implicit exploration）的新探索策略，该策略在计算和信息论层面均优于以往方法。

Result: 新算法在部分可观测的在线学习问题中实现了近最优的遗憾界，且在组合优化场景下保持了计算高效性。隐式探索策略展现出比先前探索策略更优的计算与信息效率。

Conclusion: 隐式探索策略为处理部分可观测在线学习问题提供了一种通用且高效的新范式，填补了全信息反馈与bandit反馈之间多种场景的理论空白。

Abstract: We consider online learning problems under a partial observability model capturing situations where the information conveyed to the learner is between full information and bandit feedback. In the simplest variant, we assume that in addition to its own loss, the learner also gets to observe losses of some other actions. The revealed losses depend on the learner's action and a directed observation system chosen by the environment. For this setting, we propose the first algorithm that enjoys near-optimal regret guarantees without having to know the observation system before selecting its actions. Along similar lines, we also define a new partial information setting that models online combinatorial optimization problems where the feedback received by the learner is between semi-bandit and full feedback. As the predictions of our first algorithm cannot be always computed efficiently in this setting, we propose another algorithm with similar properties and with the benefit of always being computationally efficient, at the price of a slightly more complicated tuning mechanism. Both algorithms rely on a novel exploration strategy called implicit exploration, which is shown to be more efficient both computationally and information-theoretically than previously studied exploration strategies for the problem.

</details>


### [338] [Uncovering Latent Patterns in Social Media Usage and Mental Health: A Clustering-Based Approach Using Unsupervised Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24611)
*Md All Shahria,Sanjeda Dewan Mithila,Touhid Alam,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,Mahfuza Khatun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究使用K-Means聚类（6个簇，轮廓系数0.32）对551名参与者的社交媒体使用与心理健康数据进行分析，发现社交媒体使用时间与焦虑存在0.28的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注社交媒体使用与心理健康的相关性，但缺乏使用无监督机器学习对用户进行行为与心理模式分群的研究，难以识别不同群体的风险特征。

Method: 收集551名参与者在线问卷数据，采用KNN插补处理缺失值、独热编码处理分类变量、IQR和Z-score检测异常值；使用K-Means聚类（肘部法确定6个簇，轮廓系数0.32），PCA降维22个特征，并绘制相关热力图。

Result: 聚类优化为6个簇，轮廓系数0.32；PCA将22维数据降维可视化；相关热力图显示社交媒体使用时间与焦虑的相关系数为0.28。

Conclusion: 无监督聚类方法能够有效识别社交媒体用户的不同行为与心理健康模式，为后续差异化干预提供依据。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of social media has heightened interest in its psychological effects, particularly on mental health indicators such as anxiety, depression, loneliness, and sleep quality, as these platforms increasingly influence social interactions and well-being. Although previous research has examined correlations between social media use and mental health, few studies have utilized unsupervised machine learning to segment users based on behavioral and psychological patterns, leaving a gap in identifying distinct risk profiles across diverse groups. This study seeks to address this by segmenting individuals according to their social media usage and psychological well-being, employing clustering to reveal hidden patterns and evaluate their mental health implications. Data from 551 participants, collected via an online survey, were preprocessed using KNN imputation for missing values, one-hot encoding for categorical variables like Gender with 5 unique values, and outlier detection via IQR and Z-score methods. K-Means clustering, optimized at 6 clusters using the Elbow Method and a Silhouette Score of 0.32, was applied, with PCA reducing 22 dimensions for visualization and a correlation heatmap highlighting relationships, such as a 0.28 correlation between social media hours and anxiety.

</details>


### [339] [Diffusion-Guided Feature Selection via Nishimori Temperature: Noise-Based Spectral Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24692)
*Vasiliy S. Usatyuk,Denis A. Sapozhnikov,Sergey I. Egorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理启发的噪声谱嵌入(NBSE)方法，用于高维数据的特征选择，无需贪心搜索，在ImageNet嵌入上压缩至30%特征时准确率下降不到1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征选择方法多依赖贪心搜索或统计检验，难以处理高维数据中的冗余特征和维度灾难问题，且缺乏对噪声鲁棒性的理论保证。

Method: 1) 构建样本间稀疏相似图；2) 找到Bethe Hessian矩阵奇异的临界逆温度(西森温度)；3) 利用对应最小特征向量捕捉度矫正扩散过程的主导模态；4) 转置数据矩阵后在特征空间应用NBSE得到一维谱嵌入；5) 通过平衡分箱每组选出一个代表特征。

Result: 在MobileNetV2和EfficientNet-B4的ImageNet嵌入上，NBSE在压缩至30%特征时对EfficientNet-B4准确率下降<1%，比ANOVA F检验和随机选择高6.8%。

Conclusion: NBSE是一种鲁棒且高效的无贪心搜索特征选择方法，有理论噪声保证，在极端压缩下仍能保持分类性能。

Abstract: We propose Noise-Based Spectral Embedding (NBSE), a physics-informed framework for selecting informative features from high-dimensional data without greedy search. NBSE constructs a sparse similarity graph on the samples and identifies the Nishimori temperature $β_N$ the critical inverse temperature at which the Bethe Hessian becomes singular. The corresponding smallest eigenvector captures the dominant mode of an intrinsically degree-corrected diffusion process, naturally reweighting nodes to prevent hub dominance. By transposing the data matrix and applying NBSE in feature space, we obtain a one-dimensional spectral embedding that reveals groups of redundant or semantically related dimensions; balanced binning then selects one representative per group. We prove that coloured Gaussian perturbations shift $β_N$ by at most $O(\barσ^2)$, guaranteeing robustness to measurement noise. Experiments on ImageNet embeddings from MobileNetV2 and EfficientNet-B4 show that NBSE preserves classification accuracy even under aggressive compression: on EfficientNet-B4 the accuracy drop is below $1\%$ when retaining only $30\%$ of features, outperforming ANOVA $F$-test and random selection by up to $6.8\%$.

</details>


### [340] [Scalable Hyperparameter-Divergent Ensemble Training with Automatic Learning Rate Exploration for Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24708)
*Hailing Cheng,Tao Huang,Chen Zhu,Antonio Alonso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HDET方法，利用数据并行训练中的多个GPU副本探索不同的学习率配置，通过交替的扇出（独立训练）和收敛（参数平均）阶段，以及自动学习率控制器，实现自适应的学习率调度，无需额外超参数搜索和训练预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据并行训练中N个GPU副本计算几乎相同的梯度更新，浪费了探索丰富学习率配置空间的机会。

Method: HDET包含两个交替阶段：扇出阶段（副本在对称分布的学习率下独立训练）和收敛阶段（每T步通过AllReduce平均参数）。进一步提出自动学习率控制器，利用副本间相对训练损失作为性能信号，通过基于动量的无梯度元更新指导共享基调度。

Result: 该方法能产生自适应的学习率调度，提升优化质量和泛化能力，无需额外超参数搜索或训练预算。可推广到dropout率、注意力温度、权重衰减等其他标量超参数。

Conclusion: HDET是PyTorch OneCycleLR调度器的即插即用替代品，无需修改模型架构、优化器或数据流水线，即可实现自适应超参数探索。

Abstract: Training large neural networks with data-parallel stochastic gradient descent allocates N GPU replicas to compute effectively identical updates -- a practice that leaves the rich space of learning rate configurations entirely unexplored during training. We propose Hyperparameter-Divergent Ensemble Training (HDET), a method that repurposes these replicas for simultaneous learning rate exploration at negligible communication overhead. HDET operates in alternating phases: a fan-out stage in which replicas train independently under a structured, symmetric spread of learning rates, and a converge stage in which parameters are averaged across all replicas via AllReduce every T steps. Building on this ensemble substrate, we further propose an automatic learning rate (auto-LR) controller that treats the relative training loss across replicas as a performance signal, updating the shared base schedule toward higher-performing configurations via a momentum-based gradient-free meta-update. The combined method produces a self-adapting learning rate schedule that improves both optimization quality and generalization without additional hyperparameter sweeps or training budget.
  Crucially, the framework generalizes beyond learning rate: any scalar hyperparameter that does not alter model architecture -- such as dropout rate, attention scale temperature, or weight-decay coefficient -- can be explored across replicas using the same fan-out/converge protocol, with inter-replica loss differences serving as zero-order hypergradients that guide the search direction. HDET is implemented as a drop-in replacement for PyTorch's OneCycleLR scheduler, requiring no changes to model architecture, optimizer, or data pipeline.

</details>


### [341] [SpecRLBench: A Benchmark for Generalization in Specification-Guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24729)
*Zijian Guo,İlker Işık,H. M. Sabbir Ahmad,Wenchao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpecRLBench基准，用于评估基于LTL的规范引导强化学习方法在导航和操作任务中的泛化能力，涵盖多种难度、环境动态性和观察模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LTL引导的强化学习方法在不同规范和多样化环境下的泛化能力尚不明确，缺乏系统性的评估基准。

Method: 构建了SpecRLBench基准，包含导航和操作领域多个难度级别，涉及静态/动态环境、不同的机器人动力学和观察模态，并通过大量实验评估现有方法。

Result: 揭示了现有方法在规范和环境复杂度增加时出现的局限性，系统性地表征了其优势与不足。

Conclusion: SpecRLBench为规范引导强化学习方法的泛化能力提供了结构化评估平台，有助于推动更具泛化性的方法开发。

Abstract: Specification-guided reinforcement learning (RL) provides a principled framework for encoding complex, temporally extended tasks using formal specifications such as linear temporal logic (LTL). While recent methods have shown promising results, their ability to generalize across unseen specifications and diverse environments remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we introduce SpecRLBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate the generalization capabilities of LTL-based specification-guided RL methods. The benchmark spans multiple difficulty levels across navigation and manipulation domains, incorporating both static and dynamic environments, diverse robot dynamics, and varied observation modalities. Through extensive empirical evaluation, we characterize the strengths and limitations of existing approaches and reveal the challenges that emerge as specification and environment complexity increase. SpecRLBench provides a structured platform for systematic comparison and supports the development of more generalizable specification-guided RL methods. Code is available at https://github.com/BU-DEPEND-Lab/SpecRLBench.

</details>


### [342] [Learning to Think from Multiple Thinkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24737)
*Nirmit Joshi,Roey Magen,Nathan Srebro,Nikolaos Tsilivis,Gal Vardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多思维链（CoT）监督下的学习问题，发现在被动数据收集场景下，即使来自多个正确但系统不同的思维者，学习可能变得困难；但提出了一种高效的主动学习算法，能以少量CoT数据和适量被动结果数据实现有效学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索当多个思维者提供正确但系统不同的CoT监督时，学习难度是否会发生变化，以及在被动数据收集场景下是否存在学习瓶颈。

Method: 基于密码学假设，分析多思维者CoT监督下的学习复杂度；并提出一种通用的主动学习算法，结合少量CoT数据与大量被动端到端结果数据。

Result: 在密码学假设下，被动数据收集场景中，来自两个或少数不同思维者的CoT监督可能导致学习困难；而主动学习算法只需与精度无关的少量CoT数据、对数级别的思维者数量以及多项式级别的被动结果数据即可高效学习。

Conclusion: 多思维者CoT监督在被动场景下可能带来学习困难，但通过主动学习策略可以克服这一挑战，实现高效学习。

Abstract: We study learning with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) supervision from multiple thinkers, all of whom provide correct but possibly systematically different solutions, e.g., step-by-step solutions to math problems written by different thinkers, or step-by-step execution traces of different programs solving the same problem.
  We consider classes that are computationally easy to learn using CoT supervision from a single thinker, but hard to learn with only end-result supervision, i.e., without CoT (Joshi et al. 2025). We establish that, under cryptographic assumptions, learning can be hard from CoT supervision provided by two or a few different thinkers, in passive data-collection settings.
  On the other hand, we provide a generic computationally efficient active learning algorithm that learns with a small amount of CoT data per thinker that is completely independent of the target accuracy $\varepsilon$, a moderate number of thinkers that scales as $\log \frac{1}{\varepsilon}\log \log \frac{1}{\varepsilon}$, and sufficient passive end-result data that scales as $\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\cdot poly\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}$.

</details>


### [343] [The Optimal Sample Complexity of Multiclass and List Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24749)
*Chirag Pabbaraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了多类假设类的最大超图密度不超过其DS维数，解决了Daniely和Shalev-Shwartz（2014）的长期猜想，从而确定了多类分类和列表学习在样本复杂度上对DS维数的最优依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多类分类的最优样本复杂度长期悬而未决，上下界之间存在√DS的差距，需要确定DS维数是否真正刻画了样本复杂度的最优依赖关系。

Method: 基于Hanneke等人（2026）关于多类假设类的代数刻画，通过证明最大超图密度上界为DS维数，建立超图密度与DS维数的联系。

Result: 证明了最大超图密度≤DS维数，解决了Daniely和Shalev-Shwartz（2014）的猜想，从而确定了样本复杂度对DS维数的最优依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该结果闭合了多类分类样本复杂度的理论空白，同时将结论推广到列表学习场景，为后续研究提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: While the optimal sample complexity of binary classification in terms of the VC dimension is well-established, determining the optimal sample complexity of multiclass classification has remained open. The appropriate complexity parameter for multiclass classification is the DS dimension, and despite significant efforts, a gap of $\sqrt{\text{DS}}$ has persisted between the upper and lower bounds on sample complexity.
  Recent work by Hanneke et al. (2026) shows a novel algebraic characterization of multiclass hypothesis classes in terms of their DS dimension. Building up on this, we show that the maximum hypergraph density of any multiclass hypothesis class is upper-bounded by its DS dimension. This proves a longstanding conjecture of Daniely and Shalev-Shwartz (2014). As a consequence, we determine the optimal dependence of the sample complexity on the DS dimension for multiclass as well as list learning.

</details>
